<script type="text/javascript">
            (function() {
              if (!Object.hasOwn) {
                Object.hasOwn = function(obj, prop) {
                  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(Object(obj), prop);
                };
              }
              if (!Array.prototype.at) {
                Array.prototype.at = function(index) {
                  var len = this.length;
                  var relativeIndex = Math.trunc(index) || 0;
                  if (relativeIndex < 0) relativeIndex += len;
                  return relativeIndex < 0 || relativeIndex >= len ? undefined : this[relativeIndex];
                };
              }
              if (!String.prototype.at) {
                String.prototype.at = function(index) {
                  var len = this.length;
                  var relativeIndex = Math.trunc(index) || 0;
                  if (relativeIndex < 0) relativeIndex += len;
                  return relativeIndex < 0 || relativeIndex >= len ? undefined : this.charAt(relativeIndex);
                };
              }
            })();
          </script>
<script type="text/javascript">
            (function() {
            if (typeof document !== 'undefined'){
            function getCookie(name) {
                const value = `; ${document.cookie}`;
                const parts = value.split(`; ${name}=`);
                if (parts.length === 2) return parts.pop().split(';').shift();
              }
              const darkModeCookie = getCookie('cre-theme')
              const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')
              if (darkModeCookie && darkModeCookie !== 'system') {
                document.documentElement.classList.add(darkModeCookie)
              } else {
                const mode = mediaQuery.matches ? 'dark':'light'
                document.documentElement.classList.add(mode)
                // document.cookie = `cre-theme=${mode}; path=/`
              }
            }
            })();
          </script>
